# Title: HaGeZi's Multi LIGHT - basic protection
# Description: Hand brush edition. Cleans up the internet and protects your privacy without going overboard. Blocks ads, trackers, metrics, telemetry, and some badware. Basically a size-optimized version of Multi NORMAL, built only from domains that appear on Top 1M/10M lists.
# Note: This version shouldn't cause any real restrictions. Great if there's no admin around to unblock stuff for you, or if your ad blocker chokes on big lists.
